2723function paintSubs(){
2724  const host = document.getElementById('subsAll');
2725  if (!host) return;
2726
2727  /* one flat list, each entry carrying its own status */
2728  const all = []
2729    .concat(Object.entries(LIVE_PC).map(([pc,nm]) => ({pc, nm, st:'live'})))
2730    .concat(Object.entries(SOON_PC).map(([pc,nm]) => ({pc, nm, st:'soon'})));
2731
2732  const lgL = document.getElementById('lgLive');
2733  const lgS = document.getElementById('lgSoon');
2734  if (lgL) lgL.textContent = '(' + all.filter(s=>s.st==='live').length + ' suburbs)';
2735  if (lgS) lgS.textContent = '(' + all.filter(s=>s.st==='soon').length + ' suburbs)';
2736
2737  /* group by region, ordered */
2738  const groups = {};
2739  all.forEach(s => { const r = PC_REGION[s.pc] || 'Other'; (groups[r] = groups[r] || []).push(s); });
2740  const order = REGION_ORDER.filter(r => groups[r]).concat(Object.keys(groups).filter(r => !REGION_ORDER.includes(r)));
2741
2742  host.innerHTML = order.map(r => {
2743    const list = groups[r].sort((a,b) => a.nm.localeCompare(b.nm));
2744    const nLive = list.filter(s=>s.st==='live').length;
2745    const note = nLive === list.length ? 'All collecting now'
2746               : nLive === 0 ? 'Opening next'
2747               : nLive + ' of ' + list.length + ' collecting now';
2748    return '<div class="rgrp">'
2749      + '<div class="rgrp-h"><h3>' + r + '</h3><span>' + note + '</span></div>'
2750      + '<div class="subs">' + list.map(s =>
2751          '<button class="sub' + (s.st==='soon'?' is-soon':'') + '" data-pc="' + s.pc + '">'
2752          + '<span>' + s.nm + '</span>'
2753          + '<span class="sub-pc">' + s.pc + '<span class="dot ' + s.st + '"></span></span>'
2754          + '</button>').join('')
2755      + '</div></div>';
2756  }).join('');
2757
2758  /* tapping a suburb runs it through the checker */
2759  host.querySelectorAll('[data-pc]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
2760    const f = document.getElementById('covPc2');
2761    if (!f) return;
2762    f.value = b.dataset.pc;
2763    document.getElementById('covGo2').click();
2764    document.querySelector('#p-coverage .cov').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'});
2765  });
2766}

2768/* ---------- COVERAGE CHECK ---------- */
2769function checkPc(inp, res, showForm){
2770  const v = (document.getElementById(inp).value || '').trim();
2771  const el = document.getElementById(res);
2772  el.className = 'cov-r show';
2773  if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(v)){ el.classList.add('soon'); el.textContent = 'Please enter a valid four-digit Australian postcode.'; return; }
2774  if (LIVE_PC[v]){
2775    el.classList.add('ok');
2776    el.innerHTML = 'We are running collections in ' + LIVE_PC[v] + ' (' + v + '). <a href="#quote">Get a quote and book a collection.</a>';
2777    if (showForm) document.getElementById('covForm').style.display = 'none';
2778  } else if (SOON_PC[v]){
2779    el.classList.add('soon');
2780    el.innerHTML = SOON_PC[v] + ' (' + v + ') is next on our list — we are recruiting an operator for that round now. Register below and we will contact you before we launch.';
2781    if (showForm){ document.getElementById('covForm').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('covSuburb').value = SOON_PC[v]; }
2782  } else {
2783    el.classList.add('no');
2784    el.innerHTML = 'We are not in ' + v + ' yet. Register your interest below — we open new rounds where registered demand is highest.';
2785    if (showForm) document.getElementById('covForm').style.display = 'block';
2786  }
2787  el.dataset.pc = v;
2788}
2789document.getElementById('covGo').onclick = () => checkPc('covPc','covRes', true);
2790document.getElementById('covGo2').onclick = () => checkPc('covPc2','covRes2', false);
2791document.getElementById('covPc').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') checkPc('covPc','covRes', true); });
2792document.getElementById('covPc2').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') checkPc('covPc2','covRes2', false); });

2825const val = id => (document.getElementById(id)?.value || '').trim();
2826function say(id, ok, txt){ const e = document.getElementById(id); e.className = 'fmsg show ' + (ok?'ok':'err'); e.textContent = txt; e.setAttribute('role','status'); e.setAttribute('aria-live','polite'); }
2827
2828async function save(coll, data, msgId, okTxt){
2829  const rec = Object.assign({ createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), status: 'new' }, data);
2830  if (!DB){
2831    say(msgId, false, 'The enquiry system is not connected in this view. Please use the contact details in the footer.');
2832    console.warn('[LK] db unavailable — record not saved:', coll, rec);
2833    return false;
2834  }
2835  try {
2836    await DB.collection(coll).add(rec);
2837    say(msgId, true, okTxt);
2838    return true;
2839  } catch (e){
2840    say(msgId, false, e.code === 'quota_exceeded'
2841      ? 'Our enquiry store is full. Please contact us directly.'
2842      : 'Could not send that. Please try again, or contact us directly.');
2843    console.error('[LK]', e);
2844    return false;
2845  }
2846}

2848/* ---- form wiring ---- */
2849document.getElementById('qGo').onclick = async () => {
2850  const sub = val('qSub');
2851  if (!sub){ say('qMsg', false, 'Select your suburb first.'); return; }
2852  await save('quotes', {
2853    suburb: sub, service: val('qSvc'),
2854    loads: C.a,
2855    estimate: document.getElementById('qTotal').textContent,
2856    pricingSnapshot: { load: PX.load, linenSheetSet: PX.linenSheetSet, delivery: PX.delivery, minimum: PX.minimum }
2857  }, 'qMsg', 'Request received. We will contact you to confirm a collection window and your final price.');
2858};
2859document.getElementById('covSave').onclick = async () => {
2860  const pc = document.getElementById('covRes').dataset.pc || val('covPc');
2861  if (!val('covEmail')){ say('covMsg', false, 'Enter an email so we can let you know.'); return; }
2862  await save('coverage', { postcode: pc, suburb: val('covSuburb'), email: val('covEmail') },

2895/* ---------- DASHBOARD ---------- */
2896const COLLS = [
2897  ['quotes','Quote requests'], ['contact','Contact messages'], ['business','Commercial enquiries'],
2898  ['support','Supported living'], ['operators','Operator applications'], ['coverage','Coverage requests'],
2899  ['bookings_inbox','App bookings'], ['feedback','App feedback'],
2900  ['operators','Operators'], ['invoices','Invoices']
2901];
2902let dashTab = 'quotes';
2903async function loadDash(){
2904  const gate = document.getElementById('dashGate'), body = document.getElementById('dashBody');
2905  if (!DB || !CAN_EDIT){
2906    gate.style.display = ''; body.style.display = 'none';
2907    gate.innerHTML = '<h3>Not available in this view</h3><p>This dashboard is visible only to LaundryKings editors signed in to Claude. If that is you and you are seeing this, the data layer has not loaded.</p>';
2908    return;
2909  }
2910  gate.style.display = 'none'; body.style.display = '';
2911  document.getElementById('dashTabs').innerHTML = COLLS.map(([k,l]) =>
2912    '<button class="dtab' + (k===dashTab?' on':'') + '" data-tab="' + k + '">' + l + '</button>').join('');
2913  document.querySelectorAll('.dtab').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { dashTab = b.dataset.tab; loadDash(); });
2914  const list = document.getElementById('dashList');
2915  list.innerHTML = '<p style="color:var(--mute);font-size:var(--fs-micro)">Loading…</p>';
2916  try {
2917    const snap = await DB.collection(dashTab).orderBy('createdAt','desc').limit(100).get();
2918    if (snap.empty){ list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty"><h3>Nothing here yet</h3><p>Submissions to this form will appear here as they come in.</p></div>'; return; }
2919    list.innerHTML = snap.docs.map(d => {
2920      const v = d.data();
2921      const when = v.createdAt ? new Date(v.createdAt).toLocaleString('en-AU') : '';
2922      const rows = Object.entries(v).filter(([k]) => !['createdAt','status','pricingSnapshot'].includes(k))
2923        .map(([k,val2]) => '<dt>' + k + '</dt><dd>' + String(val2 ?? '') + '</dd>').join('');
2924      return '<div class="rec"><div class="rec-h"><strong>' + (v.name || v.business || v.suburb || v.email || d.id) + '</strong><time>' + when + '</time></div><dl>' + rows + '</dl></div>';
2925    }).join('');
2926  } catch(e){
2927    list.innerHTML = '<div class="empty"><h3>Could not load</h3><p>' + (e.message || 'Unknown error') + '</p></div>';
2928  }
2929}

2994async function appLoad(){
2995  if (DB && USER){
2996    try { UID = await USER.id(); } catch(e){ UID = null; }
2997  }
2998  if (DB && UID){
2999    ACCT_REF = DB.doc('data/users/' + UID + '/account');
3000    try {
3001      const snap = await ACCT_REF.get();
3002      if (snap.exists) S.acct = Object.assign(S.acct, snap.data(), { comms: Object.assign({}, S.acct.comms, (snap.data()||{}).comms) });
3003      PERSISTED = true;
3004    } catch(e){ PERSISTED = false; }
3005    if (PERSISTED){
3006      try { S.profiles  = (await sub('profiles').get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
3007      try { S.bookings  = (await sub('bookings').orderBy('date','desc').limit(100).get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
3008      try { S.messages  = (await sub('messages').orderBy('at','desc').limit(100).get()).docs.map(d => Object.assign({id:d.id}, d.data())); } catch(e){}
3009    }
3010  }
3011  if (!PERSISTED){
3012    if (MEM.acct) S.acct = MEM.acct;
3013    S.profiles = MEM.profiles; S.bookings = MEM.bookings; S.messages = MEM.messages;
3014  }
3015  if (!S.profiles.length){
3016    const p = { id:'p'+uid4(), name:'My home', contact:'', email:'', phone:'', type:'Household',
3017                line1:'', suburb:'', postcode:'', state:'QLD', access:'', care:'', del:'', svc:'wf',
3018                eco:false, preferred:true, createdAt:Date.now() };
3019    S.profiles.push(p); S.acct.activeId = p.id;
3020    if (!PERSISTED) MEM.profiles = S.profiles;
3021  }
3022  if (!S.acct.activeId) S.acct.activeId = S.profiles[0].id;
3023  APP_READY = true;
3024}

3026async function saveAcct(){
3027  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM.acct = S.acct; return true; }
3028  try { await ACCT_REF.set(S.acct); return true; } catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
3029}
3030async function saveProfile(p){
3031  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM.profiles = S.profiles; return true; }
3032  try { const {id, ...rest} = p; await sub('profiles').doc(id).set(rest); return true; }
3033  catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
3034}
3035async function pushDoc(name, rec){
3036  if (!PERSISTED){ MEM[name] = S[name]; return true; }
3037  try { await sub(name).doc(rec.id).set((({id,...r})=>r)(rec)); return true; }
3038  catch(e){ console.error('[LK]', e); return false; }
3039}
3040function addMessage(ref, title, body){
3041  const m = { id:'m'+uid4(), ref, title, at:Date.now(), unread:true,
3042              turns:[{ who:'system', text:body, at:Date.now() }] };
3043  S.messages.unshift(m); pushDoc('messages', m); return m;
3044}

3046/* ---- pricing estimate ---- */
3047function estimate(svc, a, b){
3048  if (svc === 'comm') return null;
3049  let lines = [], subtot = 0;
3050  if (svc === 'wf'   ){ const v=a*PX.load; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × wash & fold load'+(a>1?'s':''), v]); }
3051  if (svc === 'linen'){ const v=a*PX.linenSheetSet; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × blanket, rug or sheet set'+(a>1?'s':''), v]); }
3052  if (svc === 'dc'   ){ const v=a*PX.dcDress; subtot+=v; lines.push([a+' × garment'+(a>1?'s':'')+' (indicative average)', v]); }
3053  const p = activeP();
3054  if (p && p.eco){ const v = PX.ecoAddon; subtot += v; lines.push(['Fragrance-free / sensitive detergent', v]); }
3055  lines.push(['Collection & return', PX.delivery]); subtot += PX.delivery;
3056  const total = Math.max(PX.minimum, subtot);
3057  if (total > subtot + 0.001) lines.push(['Minimum order adjustment', total - subtot]);
3058  return { lines, total };
3059}
3060const sumRows = est => est.lines.map(([l,v]) => '<div class="sumr"><span>'+esc(l)+'</span><span>'+money(v)+'</span></div>').join('')
3061  + '<div class="sumr tot"><span>Estimated total</span><span>'+money(est.total)+'</span></div>';

3072    t.classList.toggle('on', k === id || (k==='messages' && id==='thread') || (k==='account' && ['profile','pdetails','prefs','address','comms','invoices','userdet','feedback','help'].includes(id)));
3073  });
3074  const n = S.messages.filter(m => m.unread).length;
3075  const b = document.getElementById('tabBadge');
3076  b.style.display = n ? '' : 'none'; b.textContent = n;
3077}

3098function bookingCard(b){
3099  const d = fromIso(b.date);
3100  const pill = b.status === 'cancelled' ? '<span class="pill cx">CANCELLED</span>'
3101             : b.status === 'complete'  ? '<span class="pill ok">COMPLETE</span>'
3102             : b.status === 'confirmed' ? '<span class="pill up">CONFIRMED</span>'
3103             : '<span class="pill pd">AWAITING OPERATOR</span>';
3104  return '<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-h"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-basket"/></svg></i>'+esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))+'<span style="margin-left:auto">'+pill+'</span></div>'
3105   + '<div class="ap-card-b"><div class="opt" style="margin:0;border:none;padding:0;background:none">'
3106   + '<div class="opt-d"><s>'+dayName(d)+'</s><b>'+d.getDate()+'</b><s>'+monName(d)+'</s></div>'
3107   + '<div class="opt-m"><strong>'+esc(b.window)+'</strong><small>'+esc(b.ref)+(b.recurring?' · repeats '+esc(b.recurring):'')+'</small></div>'
3108   + '<div class="opt-p">'+(b.estimate ? money(b.estimate) : '—')+'</div></div>'
3109   + (b.status==='requested'||b.status==='confirmed'
3110      ? '<button class="btn btn-out btn-full" style="margin-top:.9rem" data-cancel="'+b.id+'">Cancel this collection</button>' : '')
3111   + '</div></div>';
3112}

3121  if (past.length){ h += '<div class="ap-sec-t">PAST &amp; CANCELLED ('+past.length+')</div>' + past.map(bookingCard).join(''); }

3126async function cancelBooking(id){
3127  const b = S.bookings.find(x => x.id === id); if (!b) return;
3128  b.status = 'cancelled'; b.cancelledAt = Date.now();
3129  await pushDoc('bookings', b);
3130  addMessage(b.ref, b.ref + ' has been cancelled',
3131    'Your collection ' + b.ref + ' on ' + longDate(b.date) + ', ' + b.window + ', has been cancelled. No charge applies. You can rebook any time from the Book tab.');
3132  renderBookings(); renderMessages(); renderTabs('bookings');
3133}

3201/* ---- form hydration ---- */
3202function renderForms(){
3203  const p = activeP(); if (!p) return;
3204  const set = (id, v) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.value = v == null ? '' : v; };
3205  const chk = (id, v) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.checked = !!v; };
3206  set('pdName',p.name); set('pdContact',p.contact); set('pdEmail',p.email); set('pdPhone',p.phone);
3207  set('pdType',p.type); chk('pdPref',p.preferred);
3208  set('pfCare',p.care); set('pfDel',p.del); set('pfSvc',p.svc); chk('pfEco',p.eco);
3209  set('adLine1',p.line1); set('adSub',p.suburb); set('adPc',p.postcode); set('adState',p.state); set('adAccess',p.access);
3210  set('udFirst',S.acct.first); set('udLast',S.acct.last); set('udEmail',S.acct.email); set('udPhone',S.acct.phone);
3211  const eco = document.getElementById('pfEcoPx'); if (eco) eco.textContent = ' (' + money(PX.ecoAddon) + ' per collection)';
3212  document.getElementById('cmList').innerHTML = COMMS.map(([k,t,d,locked]) =>
3213    '<div class="tgl"'+(k===COMMS[0][0]?' style="border-top:none"':'')+'>'
3214    + '<div class="tgl-t">'+t+'<small>'+d+'</small></div>'
3215    + '<button class="sw'+((locked || S.acct.comms[k]) ? ' on':'')+'"'+(locked?' disabled style="opacity:.55;cursor:not-allowed"':' data-cm="'+k+'"')+'></button></div>').join('');
3216  document.querySelectorAll('[data-cm]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => b.classList.toggle('on'));
3217  renderInvoices();
3218}

3222  document.getElementById('inList').innerHTML = done.length
3223    ? done.map(b => '<div class="ap-row"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-receipt"/></svg></i><div><strong>'+esc(b.invoice)+'</strong>'
3224        + '<small>'+longDate(b.date)+' · '+esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))+'</small></div>'
3225        + '<span class="opt-p">'+money(b.charged || b.estimate)+'</span></div>').join('')

3227      + '<p>An invoice is issued after each collection is weighed and completed. Paid invoices stay here for your records.</p></div></div>';

3235function wizStep(n){

3238  [1,2,3].forEach(i => document.getElementById('pg'+i).classList.toggle('on', i<=Math.min(n,3)));
3239  const t = { 1:['Choose your service','Select what you need washed.'],
3240              2:['Service availability','Select your preferred collection window.'],
3241              3:['Review booking details','Add care instructions and confirm.'],
3242              4:['Booking requested','']}[n];
3243  document.getElementById('bkTitle').textContent = t[0];
3244  document.getElementById('bkSub').textContent = t[1];
3245  document.getElementById('bkBack').style.visibility = n>1 && n<4 ? '' : 'hidden';
3246  window.scrollTo(0,0);
3247}

3274/* ---- helpers ---- */
3275function gcalUrl(b){
3276  if (!b.date||!b.window) return '';
3277  const d = b.date.replace(/-/g,'');
3278  const wm = { '9am':['090000','120000'], '12pm':['120000','150000'], '4pm':['160000','190000'] };
3279  const key = Object.keys(wm).find(k => b.window.startsWith(k));
3280  const [st,en] = wm[key]||['090000','120000'];
3281  const t = encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — ${b.service||'Collection'} — ${b.contact||b.profile||''}`);
3282  const l = encodeURIComponent(b.address||b.suburb||'');
3283  const dt = encodeURIComponent(`Ref: ${b.ref}\nCustomer: ${b.contact||b.profile||''}\nPhone: ${b.phone||''}\nCare: ${b.care||'Standard'}\nDelivery: ${b.delivery||'Safe place'}`);
3284  return `https://calendar.google.com/calendar/render?action=TEMPLATE&text=${t}&dates=${d}T${st}/${d}T${en}&location=${l}&details=${dt}`;
3285}
3286function mailtoConfirm(b, extra=''){
3287  const subj = encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — Collection confirmed · ${b.ref||''}`);
3288  const bod  = encodeURIComponent(
3289    `Hi ${b.contact||'there'},\n\nYour LaundryKings collection is confirmed!\n\n`
3290    +`Reference: ${b.ref||''}\nService: ${b.service||''}\nDate: ${b.date||''} · ${b.window||''}\nAddress: ${b.address||b.suburb||''}\n`
3291    +(b.operatorName?`Operator: ${b.operatorName}\n`:'')
3292    +`\nPlease have your laundry bagged and ready at the door before the start of your window.\n`
3293    +`Your operator will weigh and count everything, then confirm the final price before starting.\n`
3294    +(extra?`\n${extra}\n`:'')
3295    +`\nQuestions? Reply to this email or message us through the app.\n\nThank you!\nThe LaundryKings Team`);
3296  return `mailto:${encodeURIComponent(b.email||'')}?subject=${subj}&body=${bod}`;
3297}

3299/* ---- ops data load ---- */
3300async function opsLoad(){
3301  if (!DB||!CAN_EDIT) return;
3302  try { OPS.jobs = (await DB.collection('bookings_inbox').orderBy('createdAt','desc').limit(300).get()).docs.map(d=>Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data())); } catch(e){ OPS.jobs=[]; }
3303  try { OPS.operators = (await DB.collection('operators').get()).docs.map(d=>Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data())).filter(o=>o.active!==false); } catch(e){ OPS.operators=[]; }
3304  try {
3305    const pc = await DB.doc('config/payment').get();
3306    if (pc.exists){
3307      const pd = pc.data();
3308      if (pd.payid) document.getElementById('invPayId').textContent = `PayID: ${pd.payid}${pd.bsb?' · BSB: '+pd.bsb+' Acct: '+pd.account:''}`;
3309      if (pd.stripeLink){ const a=document.getElementById('invStripe'); a.innerHTML = `Stripe payment link: <a href="${esc(pd.stripeLink)}" target="_blank" style="color:var(--navy);text-decoration:underline">${esc(pd.stripeLink)}</a>`; }
3310    }
3311  } catch(e){}
3312}
3313async function saveJob(job){
3314  if (!DB||!CAN_EDIT) return false;
3315  const {id,...rest}=job; try{ await DB.collection('bookings_inbox').doc(id).set(rest); return true; }catch(e){ console.error('[LK ops]',e); return false; }
3316}

3319function renderOps(){

3327      <div class="kpi-c"><h4>${n('assigned')}</h4><p><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-clipboard"/></svg> Assigned to operator</p></div>
3328      <div class="kpi-c"><h4>${n('collected')}</h4><p><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-basket"/></svg> Collected — invoice pending</p></div>
3329      <div class="kpi-c ok"><h4>${n('complete')}</h4><p><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check-circle"/></svg> Complete (all time)</p></div>
3330    </div>
3331    <div class="ops-otabs" id="opsTabs"></div>
3332    <div id="opsQueue"></div>`;
3333
3334  document.getElementById('opsTabs').innerHTML = OPS_TAB_DEF.map(t=>
3335    `<button class="ops-otab${opsTab===t.id?' on':''}" data-otab="${t.id}">${t.label} <span style="opacity:.58">(${all.filter(t.filter).length})</span></button>`
3336  ).join('');
3337  document.querySelectorAll('[data-otab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{ opsTab=b.dataset.otab; renderOps(); });
3338

3340  const filtered = all.filter(cur.filter);

3342    ? filtered.map(opsCard).join('')
3343    : `<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check-circle"/></svg></i><h3>Nothing in "${cur.label}"</h3></div></div>`;
3344
3345  wireOpsActions();

3348function opsCard(b){
3349  const stCls = {requested:'new',assigned:'assigned',collected:'collected',invoiced:'invoiced',complete:'',cancelled:''}[b.status]||'';
3350  const pill = `<span class="pill ${({requested:'pd',assigned:'up',collected:'pd',invoiced:'ok',complete:'ok',cancelled:'cx'}[b.status]||'pd')}">${OPS_LABELS[b.status]||b.status}</span>`;
3351  const cal = gcalUrl(b);
3352  const opEl = b.operatorName
3353    ? `<div class="bkc-op"><div class="bkc-av">${esc(initials(b.operatorName))}</div>${esc(b.operatorName)}</div>`
3354    : OPS.operators.length
3355      ? `<div class="selw" style="min-width:150px"><select class="sel" style="font-size:var(--fs-micro);padding:.34rem .6rem" data-opassign="${b.id}"><option value="">Assign operator…</option>${OPS.operators.map(o=>`<option value="${o.id}">${esc(o.first+' '+o.last)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`
3356      : `<a href="#/opsops" style="font-size:var(--fs-micro);color:var(--navy);text-decoration:underline">Add operators <svg class="ic ic-inl ic-aft" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-arrow-right"/></svg></a>`;
3357
3358  const acts = [];
3359  if (b.status==='requested')  acts.push(['btn-red','confirm|'+b.id,'<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check"/></svg> Confirm']);
3360  if (b.status==='assigned')   acts.push(['btn-nv','collect|'+b.id,'Mark collected']);
3361  if (b.status==='collected')  acts.push(['btn-red','invoice|'+b.id,'<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-receipt"/></svg> Invoice']);
3362  if (b.status==='invoiced')   acts.push(['btn-ok','paid|'+b.id,'<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check-circle"/></svg> Mark paid']);
3363  acts.push(['btn-out','detail|'+b.id,'Detail <svg class="ic ic-sm" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-chevron-right"/></svg>']);
3364
3365  return `<div class="ops-c ${stCls}">
3366    <div class="ops-ch"><div><div class="ops-ref">${esc(b.ref||b.id)}</div><div class="ops-sub">${esc(b.service||'')}${b.recurring?' · recurring':''}${b.estimate?' · est. '+money(b.estimate):''}</div></div>${pill}</div>
3367    <div class="ops-b">
3368      <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-user"/></svg></i><span><strong>${esc(b.contact||b.profile||'Customer')}</strong>${b.email?` · ${esc(b.email)}`:''}${b.phone?` · ${esc(b.phone)}`:''}</span></div>
3369      <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-calendar"/></svg></i><span>${esc(b.date||'')} · ${esc(b.window||'')}${cal?' · <a href="'+cal+'" target="_blank">+ Calendar</a>':''}</span></div>
3370      <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-pin"/></svg></i><span>${esc(b.address||b.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3371      ${b.care?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-shirt"/></svg></i><span style="color:var(--mute)">${esc(b.care)}</span></div>`:''}
3372      ${b.delivery?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-truck"/></svg></i><span style="color:var(--mute)">${esc(b.delivery)}</span></div>`:''}
3373      <div class="ops-obar">${opEl}<a href="${mailtoConfirm(b)}" style="font-size:var(--fs-micro);color:var(--navy);text-decoration:underline"><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-mail"/></svg> Email customer</a></div>
3374    </div>
3375    <div class="ops-acts2">${acts.map(([cls,act,lbl])=>`<button class="btn ${cls}" data-opact="${act}">${lbl}</button>`).join('')}</div>
3376  </div>`;
3377}
3378
3379function wireOpsActions(){
3380  document.querySelectorAll('[data-opact]').forEach(btn=>{
3381    btn.onclick=()=>{
3382      const [act,id]=btn.dataset.opact.split('|');
3383      const job=OPS.jobs.find(b=>b.id===id); if(!job) return;
3384      if(act==='detail') { opsCurJob=job; location.hash='#/opsbook'; }
3385      if(act==='confirm') confirmOpsJob(job);
3386      if(act==='collect') collectOpsJob(job);
3387      if(act==='paid')    paidOpsJob(job);
3388      if(act==='invoice') { opsCurJob=job; renderInvPage(job); location.hash='#/opsinv'; }
3389    };
3390  });
3391  document.querySelectorAll('[data-opassign]').forEach(sel=>{
3392    sel.onchange=()=>{
3393      const job=OPS.jobs.find(b=>b.id===sel.dataset.opassign); if(!job) return;
3394      const op=OPS.operators.find(o=>o.id===sel.value); if(!op) return;
3395      assignOpsOperator(job,op);
3396    };
3397  });
3398}

3400async function confirmOpsJob(job){ job.status='assigned'; job.confirmedAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3401async function collectOpsJob(job){ job.status='collected'; job.collectedAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3402async function paidOpsJob(job){ job.status='complete'; job.paidAt=Date.now(); await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps(); }
3403async function assignOpsOperator(job,op){
3404  Object.assign(job,{operatorId:op.id,operatorName:op.first+' '+op.last,operatorEmail:op.email||'',operatorPhone:op.phone||''});
3405  if(job.status==='requested') job.status='assigned';
3406  job.confirmedAt=Date.now();
3407  await saveJob(job); await opsLoad(); renderOps();
3408}

3410/* ---- job detail ---- */
3411function renderOpsBook(){
3412  const job=opsCurJob;
3413  if(!job){ document.getElementById('ojBody').innerHTML='<div class="ap-note warn"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-alert"/></svg></i><span>Job not found.</span></div>'; return; }
3414  document.getElementById('ojRef').textContent=job.ref||job.id;
3415  document.getElementById('ojStatus').textContent=OPS_LABELS[job.status]||job.status;
3416  const cal=gcalUrl(job);
3417  const STAGES=['requested','assigned','collected','invoiced','complete'];
3418  const cur=STAGES.indexOf(job.status);
3419  const timeline=STAGES.map((st,i)=>{
3420    const done=i<cur, active=i===cur;
3421    return `<div class="stnode"><div class="stnode-ic ${done?'done':active?'cur':''}">${done?'<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check"/></svg>':i+1}</div><div class="stnode-lbl ${done?'done':active?'cur':''}">${OPS_LABELS[st]}</div></div>`+(i<STAGES.length-1?`<div class="stline ${i<cur?'done':''}"></div>`:'');
3422  }).join('');
3423  const opSel=OPS.operators.length
3424    ?`<div class="fld"><label class="lbl">Assign / change operator</label><div class="selw"><select class="sel" id="ojOpSel" aria-label="Assign / change operator"><option value="">Select operator…</option>${OPS.operators.map(o=>`<option value="${o.id}"${job.operatorId===o.id?' selected':''}>${esc(o.first+' '+o.last)} · ${esc(o.suburb||'')}</option>`).join('')}</select></div></div><button class="btn btn-nv btn-full" id="ojAssign" style="margin-top:.55rem">Assign selected</button>`
3425    :`<p class="ap-fine">No operators registered. <a href="#/opsops" style="color:var(--navy)">Add operators <svg class="ic ic-inl ic-aft" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-arrow-right"/></svg></a></p>`;
3426  const acts=[];
3427  if(job.status==='requested')  acts.push(['btn-red','confirm','<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check"/></svg> Confirm booking']);
3428  if(job.status==='assigned')   acts.push(['btn-nv','collect','Mark collected']);
3429  if(job.status==='collected')  acts.push(['btn-red','invoice','<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-receipt"/></svg> Generate invoice']);
3430  if(job.status==='invoiced')   acts.push(['btn-ok','paid','<svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-check-circle"/></svg> Mark paid']);
3431  if(['requested','assigned'].includes(job.status)) acts.push(['btn-out','cancel','Cancel job']);
3432
3433  document.getElementById('ojBody').innerHTML=`
3434    <div class="ap-card"><div class="status-line">${timeline}</div>
3435      <div class="ops-b">
3436        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-user"/></svg></i><span><strong>${esc(job.contact||job.profile||'Customer')}</strong></span></div>
3437        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-mail"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.email||'—')}</span></div>
3438        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-phone"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.phone||'—')}</span></div>
3439        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-basket"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.service||'')}${job.recurring?' (recurring '+job.recurring+')':''}</span></div>
3440        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-calendar"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.date||'')} · ${esc(job.window||'')}${cal?' <a href="'+cal+'" target="_blank">Open in Calendar</a>':''}</span></div>
3441        <div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-pin"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.address||job.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3442        ${job.care?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-shirt"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.care)}</span></div>`:''}
3443        ${job.delivery?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-truck"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.delivery)}</span></div>`:''}
3444        ${job.operatorName?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-worker"/></svg></i><span>${esc(job.operatorName)} · ${esc(job.operatorEmail||'')} · ${esc(job.operatorPhone||'')}</span></div>`:''}
3445        ${job.invoiceRef?`<div class="ops-ln"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-receipt"/></svg></i><span>Invoice: ${esc(job.invoiceRef)} · ${job.invoiceTotal?money(job.invoiceTotal):''}</span></div>`:''}
3446        <div class="ops-ln" style="margin-top:.4rem"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-mail"/></svg></i><a href="${mailtoConfirm(job)}">Send confirmation email</a></div>
3447      </div>
3448    </div>
3449    <div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b">${opSel}<div class="fmsg" id="ojMsg"></div></div></div>
3450    <div class="ops-acts2">${acts.map(([cls,act,lbl])=>`<button class="btn ${cls}" data-ojact="${act}">${lbl}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
3451
3452  if(document.getElementById('ojAssign')){
3453    document.getElementById('ojAssign').onclick=async()=>{
3454      const op=OPS.operators.find(o=>o.id===document.getElementById('ojOpSel').value);
3455      if(!op){ say('ojMsg',false,'Select an operator.'); return; }
3456      await assignOpsOperator(job,op); say('ojMsg',true,'Assigned: '+op.first+' '+op.last);
3457    };
3458  }
3459  document.querySelectorAll('[data-ojact]').forEach(btn=>{
3460    btn.onclick=()=>{
3461      const act=btn.dataset.ojact;
3462      if(act==='confirm') { confirmOpsJob(job); history.back(); }
3463      if(act==='collect') { collectOpsJob(job); history.back(); }
3464      if(act==='paid')    { paidOpsJob(job); history.back(); }
3465      if(act==='invoice') { renderInvPage(job); location.hash='#/opsinv'; }
3466      if(act==='cancel')  { if(confirm('Cancel this job? This cannot be undone.')){ job.status='cancelled'; job.cancelledAt=Date.now(); saveJob(job).then(()=>{ opsLoad().then(()=>{ location.hash='#/ops'; }); }); } }
3467    };
3468  });
3469}

3471/* ---- operators management ---- */
3472function renderOpsOps(){
3473  document.getElementById('opsList').innerHTML=OPS.operators.length
3474    ?OPS.operators.map(o=>`<div class="op-row"><div class="op-av2">${esc(initials(o.first+' '+o.last))}</div><div><strong>${esc(o.first+' '+o.last)}</strong><small>${esc(o.email||'')}${o.phone?' · '+esc(o.phone):''}${o.suburb?' · '+esc(o.suburb):''}${o.abn?' · ABN '+esc(o.abn):''}</small></div><span class="pill ${o.active!==false?'ok':'cx'}">${o.active!==false?'ACTIVE':'INACTIVE'}</span></div>`).join('')
3475    :'<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-worker"/></svg></i><h3>No operators yet</h3><p>Add your first operator below.</p></div></div>';
3476
3477  document.getElementById('opGo').onclick=async()=>{
3478    if(!val('opFirst')){ say('opMsg',false,'First name is required.'); return; }
3479    const op={ first:val('opFirst'), last:val('opLast'), email:val('opEmail'), phone:val('opPhone'), suburb:val('opSuburb'), abn:val('opAbn'), active:document.getElementById('opActive').checked, createdAt:Date.now() };
3480    try{
3481      const id=val('opEditId');
3482      if(id){ await DB.collection('operators').doc(id).set(op); }
3483      else   { await DB.collection('operators').add(op); }
3484      say('opMsg',true,'Operator saved.'); await opsLoad(); renderOpsOps();
3485      ['opFirst','opLast','opEmail','opPhone','opSuburb','opAbn'].forEach(i=>{ const e=document.getElementById(i); if(e)e.value=''; });
3486      document.getElementById('opEditId').value='';
3487    }catch(e){ say('opMsg',false,'Could not save: '+e.message); }
3488  };
3489}

3492function renderInvPage(job){
3493  document.getElementById('invBack').onclick=()=>history.back();
3494  document.getElementById('invRef').textContent='Job '+esc(job.ref||job.id);
3495  document.getElementById('invWt').value='';
3496  document.getElementById('invLinen').value=job.linenItems||0;
3497  document.getElementById('invNotes').value='';
3498  document.getElementById('invAdj').value='';
3499  document.getElementById('invPreview').style.display='none';
3500  document.getElementById('invCalc').onclick=()=>calcInvoice(job);
3501  document.getElementById('invConfirm').onclick=()=>confirmInvoice(job);
3502}
3503function calcInvoice(job){
3504  const kg=parseFloat(document.getElementById('invWt').value)||0;
3505  const linenCount=parseInt(document.getElementById('invLinen').value)||0;
3506  const adj=parseFloat(document.getElementById('invAdj').value)||0;
3507  const svc=job.svc||'wf';
3508  if(!kg&&svc!=='linen'){ say('invMsg',false,'Enter the actual weight to calculate.'); return; }
3509  let lines=[],sub=0;
3510  if(svc==='wf'){
3511    const loads=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(kg/5)), v=loads*PX.load; sub+=v;
3512    lines.push([`${loads} × wash & fold load${loads>1?'s':''} (${kg} kg)`,v]);
3513  } else if(svc==='dc'){
3514    const items=Math.max(1,Math.round(kg/0.5)), v=items*PX.dcDress; sub+=v;
3515    lines.push([`${items} × garment${items>1?'s':''} (dry cleaned)`,v]);
3516  }
3517  if(svc==='linen'&&linenCount){ const v=linenCount*PX.linenSheetSet; sub+=v; lines.push([`${linenCount} × linen item at sheet set rate — use adj. field for blankets/rugs`,v]); }
3518  lines.push(['Collection & return',PX.delivery]); sub+=PX.delivery;
3519  if(adj){ lines.push([adj>=0?'Adjustment (surcharge)':'Adjustment (discount)',adj]); sub+=adj; }
3520  const total=Math.max(PX.minimum,sub);
3521  if(total>sub+0.001) lines.push(['Minimum order adjustment',total-sub]);
3522  const invNum='INV-'+Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5).toUpperCase();
3523  document.getElementById('invNum').textContent=invNum;
3524  document.getElementById('invLines').innerHTML=lines.map(([l,v])=>`<div class="sumr"><span>${esc(l)}</span><span>${money(v)}</span></div>`).join('');
3525  document.getElementById('invTotAmt').textContent=money(total);
3526  const notes=document.getElementById('invNotes').value.trim();
3527  const body=`Hi ${esc(job.contact||'there')},\n\nYour laundry is ready — here is your invoice.\n\nInvoice: ${invNum}\nBooking: ${job.ref||''}\nService: ${job.service||''}\nDate: ${job.date||''}\n${notes?'Notes: '+notes+'\n':''}\n${lines.map(([l,v])=>l.padEnd(42)+money(v)).join('\n')}\n${'─'.repeat(50)}\nTOTAL DUE: ${money(total)} (GST incl.)\n\nPay online before we can return your laundry:\n[ADD PAYMENT LINK HERE]\n\nVisual, Mastercard, Amex, Google Pay and Apple Pay accepted.\n\nQuestions? Reply to this email.\n\nThank you!\nThe LaundryKings Team`;
3528  document.getElementById('invEmailBox').innerHTML=`<strong>To:</strong> ${esc(job.email||'[email]')}<br><strong>Subject:</strong> LaundryKings — Invoice ${invNum} — ${money(total)}<br><pre>${esc(body)}</pre>`;
3529  document.getElementById('invMailto').href=`mailto:${encodeURIComponent(job.email||'')}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(`LaundryKings — Invoice ${invNum} — ${money(total)}`)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;
3530  document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.invNum=invNum;
3531  document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.total=total;
3532  document.getElementById('invPreview').style.display='';
3533  say('invMsg','','');
3534}
3535async function confirmInvoice(job){
3536  const invNum=document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.invNum;
3537  const total=parseFloat(document.getElementById('invConfirm').dataset.total)||0;
3538  job.status='invoiced'; job.invoicedAt=Date.now(); job.invoiceRef=invNum; job.invoiceTotal=total;
3539  const ok=await saveJob(job);
3540  if(ok){
3541    if(DB) try{ await DB.collection('invoices').doc(invNum).set({jobRef:job.ref,contact:job.contact||job.profile,email:job.email,service:job.service,date:job.date,total,createdAt:Date.now()}); }catch(e){}
3542    say('invMsg',true,'Invoice recorded. Job updated to awaiting payment.');
3543    await opsLoad();
3544    setTimeout(()=>{ location.hash='#/ops'; },1500);
3545  } else { say('invMsg',false,'Could not save — try again.'); }
3546}

3625const BK_FILTERS=[
3626  { id:'all',       label:'All',          fn:()=>true },
3627  { id:'upcoming',  label:'Upcoming',     fn:b=>['requested','confirmed','assigned'].includes(b.status) },
3628  { id:'complete',  label:'Complete',     fn:b=>b.status==='complete' },
3629  { id:'cancelled', label:'Cancelled',    fn:b=>b.status==='cancelled' }
3630];
3631let bkFilter='all';
3632
3633function renderBookings(){
3634  const container=document.getElementById('bkList');
3635  const all=S.bookings;
3636  const chips=`<div class="bk-filts">${BK_FILTERS.map(f=>`<button class="bk-filt${bkFilter===f.id?' on':''}${f.id==='cancelled'&&bkFilter===f.id?' ck':''}" data-bkf="${f.id}">${f.label} <span style="opacity:.62">(${all.filter(f.fn).length})</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
3637  const cur=BK_FILTERS.find(f=>f.id===bkFilter);
3638  const filtered=all.filter(cur.fn);
3639  container.innerHTML=chips+(filtered.length?filtered.map(richBookingCard).join('')
3640    :`<div class="ap-card"><div class="ap-card-b ap-empty"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-calendar"/></svg></i><h3>Nothing here</h3><p>Bookings in this category appear here.</p></div></div>`);
3641  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bkf]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{ bkFilter=b.dataset.bkf; renderBookings(); });
3642  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bkcancel]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>cancelBooking(b.dataset.bkcancel));
3643  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bkrebook]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
3644    const bk=S.bookings.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.bkrebook);
3645    if(bk){ W.svc=bk.svc||'wf'; W.win=''; W.date=''; W.step=1; location.hash='#/book'; }
3646  });
3647  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bksupport]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
3648    const bk=S.bookings.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.bksupport);
3649    if(bk){ document.getElementById('fbRef').value=bk.ref||''; location.hash='#/feedback'; }
3650  });
3651}

3653/* A booking is a promise with stages. Show where it is, not just a word. */
3654const BK_STAGES = [
3655  ['requested','Booked'], ['assigned','Assigned'], ['collected','Collected'],
3656  ['invoiced','Washed'], ['complete','Returned']
3657];
3658function bkTimeline(b){
3659  if (b.status === 'cancelled') return '';
3660  const at = BK_STAGES.findIndex(s => s[0] === b.status);
3661  const done = at < 0 ? 0 : at;
3662  return '<ol class="bk-tl" aria-label="Progress of this collection">' + BK_STAGES.map(([k,label],i) =>
3663    '<li class="' + (i < done ? 'done' : i === done ? 'now' : '') + '">'
3664    + '<span class="bk-tl-d" aria-hidden="true"></span>'
3665    + '<span class="bk-tl-l">' + label + '</span>'
3666    + (i === done ? '<span class="sr-only"> — current stage</span>' : '')
3667    + '</li>').join('') + '</ol>';
3668}
3669
3670function richBookingCard(b){
3671  const d=fromIso(b.date||isoDate(new Date()));
3672  const stMap={requested:'pd',confirmed:'up',assigned:'up',complete:'ok',cancelled:'cx',invoiced:'pd'};
3673  const stLbl={requested:'AWAITING OPERATOR',confirmed:'CONFIRMED',assigned:'CONFIRMED',complete:'COMPLETE',cancelled:'CANCELLED',invoiced:'AWAITING PAYMENT'};
3674  const pill=`<span class="pill ${stMap[b.status]||'pd'}">${stLbl[b.status]||b.status.toUpperCase()}</span>`;
3675  const borderCls=b.status==='cancelled'?'cx':b.status==='complete'?'ok':['assigned','confirmed'].includes(b.status)?'up':'';
3676  const opName=b.operatorName||'Operator TBC';
3677  const acts=[];
3678  if(['requested','assigned'].includes(b.status)) acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bkcancel="${b.id}">Cancel</button>`);
3679  if(b.status==='cancelled') acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bkrebook="${b.id}"><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-refresh"/></svg> Rebook</button>`);
3680  acts.push(`<button class="btn btn-out" data-bksupport="${b.id}"><svg class="ic ic-inl" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-headset"/></svg> Support</button>`);
3681  return `<div class="bkc ${borderCls}">
3682    <div class="bkc-h"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-basket"/></svg></i><strong>${esc(SVCNAME(b.svc))}</strong>${pill}</div>
3683    <div class="bkc-b">${bkTimeline(b)}
3684      <div class="bkc-r"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-calendar"/></svg></i><span>${d.toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'})} · ${esc(b.window||'')}</span></div>
3685      <div class="bkc-r"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-pin"/></svg></i><span>${esc(b.address||b.suburb||'')}</span></div>
3686      ${b.recurring?`<div class="bkc-r"><i class="ic"><svg class="ic" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-refresh"/></svg></i><span>Repeats ${esc(b.recurring)}</span></div>`:''}
3687      <div class="bkc-footer">
3688        <div class="bkc-op"><div class="bkc-av">LK</div>${esc(opName)}</div>
3689        <span class="opt-p" style="font-size:var(--fs-micro)">${b.invoiceTotal?money(b.invoiceTotal):b.estimate?'~'+money(b.estimate):'—'}</span>
3690      </div>
3691    </div>
3692    ${acts.length?`<div class="bkc-acts">${acts.join('')}</div>`:''}
3693  </div>`;
3694}

3711function appRoute(id){
3712  curId=id;
3713  renderTabs(id);
3714  if(!APP_READY||(!APP_ROUTES.includes(id)&&!OPS_ROUTES.includes(id))) return;
3715  if(id==='app')       renderHome();
3716  if(id==='book'){ if(W.step===4){ W.win=''; W.rec=false; W.step=1;
3717      ['bkCare','bkDel'].forEach(i=>{ const e=document.getElementById(i); if(e)e.value=''; });
3718      ['bkTos','bkCareSave','bkDelSave'].forEach(i=>{ const e=document.getElementById(i); if(e)e.checked=false; });
3719      document.getElementById('bkRec').classList.remove('on');
3720      document.getElementById('bkRecOpts').style.display='none'; wizStep(1); }
3721    renderWizard(); }
3722  if(id==='bookings')  renderBookings();
3723  if(id==='messages')  renderMessages();
3724  if(id==='thread')    renderThread();
3725  if(['account','profile'].includes(id)) renderAccount();
3726  if(['pdetails','prefs','address','comms','userdet','invoices'].includes(id)) renderForms();
3727  if(id==='ops')     { if(CAN_EDIT) opsSkeleton('opsRoot'); opsLoad().then(renderOps); }
3728  if(id==='opsbook') renderOpsBook();
3729  if(id==='opsops')  { if(CAN_EDIT) opsSkeleton('opsList'); opsLoad().then(renderOpsOps); }
3730  if(id==='opsinv'&&opsCurJob) renderInvPage(opsCurJob);
3731}

3737/* ---------- INSTALL PROMPT ----------
3738   Web-first is the promise, so the app is installable without an app store.
3739   Offered once, after a booking, and never again if dismissed. */
3740let LK_INSTALL = null;
3741window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', e => { e.preventDefault(); LK_INSTALL = e; });
3742function offerInstall(){
3743  try {
3744    if (!LK_INSTALL || localStorage.getItem('lk-install') === 'done') return;
3745  } catch(e){ if (!LK_INSTALL) return; }
3746  const bar = document.getElementById('lkInstall');
3747  if (!bar) return;
3748  bar.classList.add('show');
3749  bar.querySelector('[data-install]').onclick = async () => {
3750    bar.classList.remove('show');
3751    try { localStorage.setItem('lk-install','done'); } catch(e){}
3752    LK_INSTALL.prompt(); LK_INSTALL = null;
3753  };
3754  bar.querySelector('[data-install-no]').onclick = () => {
3755    bar.classList.remove('show');
3756    try { localStorage.setItem('lk-install','done'); } catch(e){}
3757  };
3758}

3760function wireWizard(){
3761  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
3762  if (!$('bkGo')) return;
3763
3764  /* approximate quantity */
3765  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bstep]').forEach(btn => {
3766    btn.onclick = () => {
3767      const [k, d] = btn.dataset.bstep.split(',');
3768      W[k] = Math.max(k === 'a' ? 1 : 0, Math.min(40, W[k] + Number(d)));
3769      renderWizard();
3770    };
3771  });
3772
3773  const di = $('bkDate');
3774  if (di) di.onchange = () => { W.date = di.value; W.win = ''; renderWizard(); };
3775
3776  $('bkBack').onclick = () => { if (W.step > 1 && W.step < 4) wizStep(W.step - 1); else location.hash = '#/app'; };
3777  $('bkP2').onclick   = () => wizStep(1);
3778  $('bkP3').onclick   = () => wizStep(2);
3779
3780  $('bkN1').onclick = () => {
3781    if (W.svc === 'comm'){ location.hash = '#/business'; return; }
3782    const p = activeP();
3783    if (!p || !addrLine(p)){ say('bkMsg', false, 'Add a collection address to this profile before booking.'); return; }
3784    renderWizard(); wizStep(2);
3785  };
3786
3787  $('bkN2').onclick = () => {
3788    if (!W.date){ say('bkMsg', false, 'Choose a collection date to continue.'); return; }
3789    if (!W.win){  say('bkMsg', false, 'Choose a collection window to continue.'); return; }
3790    renderWizard(); wizStep(3);
3791  };
3792
3793  /* recurring collection */
3794  const rec = $('bkRec');
3795  rec.onclick = () => {
3796    W.rec = !W.rec;
3797    rec.classList.toggle('on', W.rec);
3798    rec.setAttribute('aria-pressed', W.rec ? 'true' : 'false');
3799    $('bkRecOpts').style.display = W.rec ? '' : 'none';
3800  };
3801  const rf = $('bkRecFreq'); if (rf) rf.onchange = () => { W.freq = rf.value; };
3802
3803  /* terms gate the confirm button */
3804  const tos = $('bkTos'), go = $('bkGo');
3805  const syncTos = () => { go.disabled = !tos.checked; go.setAttribute('aria-disabled', tos.checked ? 'false' : 'true'); };
3806  tos.onchange = syncTos; syncTos();
3807
3808  go.onclick = async () => {
3809    const p = activeP();
3810    if (!tos.checked){ say('bkMsg', false, 'Please accept the terms to confirm this booking.'); return; }
3811    if (!p || !addrLine(p)){ say('bkMsg', false, 'Add a collection address before confirming.'); return; }
3812    if (!W.win){ say('bkMsg', false, 'Choose a collection window.'); return; }
3813    go.disabled = true;
3814    try {
3815      const win  = BK.windows.find(w => w.id === W.win) || { label:'' };
3816      const est  = estimate(W.svc, W.a, W.b);
3817      const care = ($('bkCare').value || '').trim();
3818      const del  = ($('bkDel').value  || '').trim();
3819      const b = {
3820        id: 'b' + uid4(),
3821        ref: REF(),
3822        status: 'requested',
3823        svc: W.svc,
3824        service: SVCNAME(W.svc),
3825        date: W.date,
3826        window: win.label,
3827        a: W.a, b: W.b,
3828        linenItems: W.svc === 'linen' ? W.a : 0,
3829        estimate: est ? est.total : 0,
3830        care: care,
3831        delivery: del,
3832        recurring: W.rec ? W.freq : '',
3833        profile: p.name,
3834        contact: p.contact || p.name,
3835        email: p.email || S.acct.email || '',
3836        phone: p.phone || S.acct.phone || '',
3837        address: addrLine(p),
3838        suburb: p.suburb || '',
3839        postcode: p.postcode || '',
3840        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
3841      };
3842
3843      S.bookings.unshift(b);
3844      await pushDoc('bookings', b);
3845
3846      /* hand the job to operations */
3847      if (DB){
3848        try { const { id, ...rest } = b; await DB.collection('bookings_inbox').doc(id).set(rest); }
3849        catch(e){ console.error('[LK] bookings_inbox', e); }
3850      }
3851
3852      /* optionally keep the notes on the profile */
3853      if ($('bkCareSave').checked || $('bkDelSave').checked){
3854        if ($('bkCareSave').checked) p.care = care;
3855        if ($('bkDelSave').checked)  p.del  = del;
3856        await saveProfile(p);
3857      }
3858
3859      addMessage(b.ref, b.ref + ' received',
3860        'We have your collection request for ' + longDate(b.date) + ', ' + b.window + '. '
3861        + 'Your operator is assigned before the day and will message you ahead of arrival. '
3862        + 'The final price is confirmed after weighing and counting, before any work begins.');
3863
3864      const tx = $('bkDoneTx');
3865      if (tx) tx.textContent = 'Reference ' + b.ref + '. We have your request for ' + longDate(b.date)
3866        + ', ' + b.window + '. You will get a message here once an operator is assigned.';
3867
3868      renderBookings(); renderMessages(); renderHome(); renderTabs('book');
3869      offerInstall();
3870      say('bkMsg', true, 'Booking ' + b.ref + ' requested.');
3871      wizStep(4);
3872    } catch(e){
3873      console.error('[LK] booking', e);
3874      say('bkMsg', false, 'Could not confirm that booking. Please try again.');
3875    } finally {
3876      go.disabled = false;
3877    }
3878  };
3879}

Method: every page was rendered to an image and inspected; every number was recomputed by script from the tokens, the rate card or the live file; every external fact was searched for a second time with different wording, and accepted only when at least two independent references agreed. Anything that fails that test carries an Unverified or Single-source tag in the body of the dossier, not just here.

Appendix E

Build record: Phases A, B and C

KEA-18 was executed on 19 September 2026 against the copy of record. This appendix records what changed, what it was verified against, and the one defect found during the build that matters more than every design defect in Part 7 combined.

The defect that was not in the audit Found during the build

The customer app and the ops layer never initialised in v4.2. appBoot() called wireWizard() and wireSettings(). Neither function existed anywhere in the 3,586-line file. appBoot threw a ReferenceError on its second statement, so every line after it never ran: the version labels, renderHome, renderAccount, renderForms, renderWizard, renderBookings, renderMessages, appRoute, the not-signed-in notice, the live config/booking and config/contact listeners, and the editor preload of operations.

Every control those two functions were meant to wire was therefore dead: the wizard's quantity steppers, Continue, Previous and Confirm, the recurring switch, the terms gate, and the settings save buttons pdGo, pfGo, adGo, udGo and cmGo, plus the profile switcher sheet. A customer could not complete a booking and could not save a profile, an address or a preference.

This corrects a claim carried in both editions of this dossier. Part 1 of v1 and v2 said "the site works end to end (booking → ops → invoice)". It did not. The functional claim was never tested; the audit that produced Part 7 was a design audit, and a design audit does not press the buttons.

ItemChange
Brand colour alignmentThe site's tokens were derived from a brand guess (red #CC2228, navy #1A3480). Sampling the logo artwork gives red #D84330 and navy #0C265F. The navy token now is the logo navy. The logo red measures 4.41:1 on white, which fails AA for small text, so it ships as --red-brand for the logo, the hero slab and non-text marks, and --red is the same hue deepened to #C43828 (5.33:1 on white, 4.81:1 on paper) for buttons and small text. Favicon, manifest, theme-colour and every inline SVG fill follow. Part 8 carries the new values; the Part 2 table is left as the record of what v4.2 shipped.
Hero image and Open Graph cardThe supplied brand photograph showed visibly soiled garments under the logo. A region-aware clean-up (tools/clean_hero.py) removed the soiling from every garment while leaving the logo pixels untouched; the result is site/brand/laundrykings-hero-clean.jpg and a 1200 × 630 crop is site/og-card.png, which the og:image and twitter:image tags now reference by absolute URL. The original is kept beside it.
Numbers that were typed, now computedThe Kings Club comparison on the home page, the pricing-page minimum in the lede, and the hero estimate all read from PX, so a config/pricing override cannot leave stale figures in copy. The saving rounds to whole dollars because the sentence says "around".
Large-linen estimateThe estimator and the ops invoice price linen at the sheet-set rate because the wizard has one quantity control; both now say so and show the three per-item rates, and the invoice points the operator at the adjustment field for blankets and rugs.
Rendering defectsThe .chk2 tick was an SVG string inside CSS content, which browsers print as text; it is now a background image. The sticky nav declared box-shadow twice; the dead declaration is gone.
Pre-flight check 7strip_js did not understand a template literal nested inside another one, so a stray word inside a string could be reported as an undefined function. It now tracks ${'{'} depth.

Interaction and motion layer (19 September 2026, commit 8d595e1)

Direction agreed with Callum before starting: evolve the existing system, whole site, visual and interaction layer only, no new features. Every addition is a token or a base-component rule, so it reaches all 34 sections without per-page edits.

LayerWhat changed
ElevationNew tokens --e-hov, --e-hov-dk, --e-press, --e-sheet, --e-toast and --lift. Rest state stays border-only (D15 stands); a soft lift and shadow appear on hover and a scale-down on press, on buttons, cards, service tiles, plan cards, chips and the coverage pill.
Motion--ease and --ease-spring replace the flat ease. Route changes fade and slide in (--tr-page, 0 s under reduced motion); wizard steps and the mobile menu slide in; FAQ answers animate their height (grid 0fr to 1fr, each answer wrapped in .ans-in); inline status messages animate in; steppers pulse the number; the sticky nav gains a shadow once scrolled; nav links carry an animated underline on hover and on the active page.
AccessibilitySkip-to-content link first in the tab order, implemented as a focus-and-scroll handler because any bare #id anchor fires this router's hashchange listener and lands on home (reproduced, then fixed; <main> renamed to main-content). FAQ buttons carry aria-expanded; say() sets role="status" and aria-live on every message it writes; the live estimate total is aria-live; inputs get a visible focus ring, not a border change alone.
VerifiedPreflight 8/8, e2e 20/20, a11y sweep 0 issues after the change. Hover and press states checked against computed box-shadow and transform, the skip link against document.activeElement, the nav against its class list; a full-page screenshot cannot show a transient pseudo-class, so none of this was taken from the CSS alone.

Layout review, both viewports (19 September 2026)

All 18 routes screenshotted at 1280 px and 390 px after the brand and content changes. No jumbled or overlapping layouts on either viewport. Three items carried forward rather than fixed, because each is a decision, not a defect:

  • Bulky Items beside Blankets, Rugs & Sheets on the Services and Pricing pages: two adjacent large-textile categories a customer may not distinguish. Options: merge into one table (recommended), or add a one-line distinction to each heading.
  • Desktop chrome in the customer app. At 1280 px the marketing top nav and the phone-style bottom tab bar are both on screen, and the app column is phone-width. Works correctly (the tab bar is fixed to the viewport; a full-page capture misplaces it, a real scroll does not) but duplicates navigation. Options: hide the marketing links inside app routes on desktop, or widen the app column. Owner Callum.
  • Callout styles. The operators page introduces a dashed-border box for "what we cannot tell you yet", a third callout style beside the warning box and the plain card. Deliberate or not, worth a decision.

Verification added to the repository

CheckWhat it provesResult
verify/preflight.pyEight checks: one executable script that parses, no duplicate IDs, 34 sections, every getElementById target exists, zero emoji, every font-size and letter-spacing value is a token, every function called is defined, and the AU compliance sentinels are still present. The seventh check is the one that would have caught the boot defect; run against the original v4.2 it reports exactly wireSettings and wireWizard.8/8 pass
verify/e2e.jsDrives a real browser through the flow a customer follows: save an address, see the coverage answer, choose a service, use the stepper, pick a window, hit the terms gate, confirm, then assert the booking record, the message and the Bookings list. Fails on any uncaught script error.20/20 pass
verify/a11y.jsWalks every visible text node on all 18 routes at 390 px, resolves the real background through the ancestor chain including alpha, and measures contrast against the 4.5:1 and 3:1 floors. Also checks accessible names and the WCAG 2.2 target-size minimum.0 issues

The accessibility sweep found three defects on its first run, two of them introduced by this build: muted text fell to 4.29:1 when the page surface changed from white to paper underneath it, and the hero eyebrow rule was scoped to the whole home page rather than the hero, so eyebrows on paper bands measured 1.36:1. The third was pre-existing: 23 form inputs across four pages had visible labels that were never associated with them, so a screen reader announced an unlabelled edit field. All three are fixed.

What has deliberately not changed

  • Pricing. Load $32, delivery $13.50 and minimum $73.50 are unchanged and still read from config/pricing. Two content decisions of 19 September 2026 sit on top of this build: ironing was withdrawn as a service (no wash-fold-iron, no iron-only) and replaced by a per-item Blankets, Rugs & Sheets service at $24 / $32 / $42; and Kings Club rose 30% to $63.70 / $89.70 / $115.70 a week. The home-page saving now computes from those live figures rather than a typed number.
  • Compliance text. Every sentinel is still present and is checked on every build: the NDIS statement, the AS/NZS 4146 statement, the contractor and ABN statements, GST-inclusive pricing and the Australian Consumer Law statement.
  • Footer entity text. Still the placeholder. D13 is Max's to supply and was never going to be invented here.
  • The live artefact. Not republished. The work is committed to the branch and waits on a principal's decision to publish.
What this changes about the plan
  1. M1 was further away than the board showed. "First job operational" assumed a working booking flow. Until this build there was not one. M1 is now genuinely reachable, and the gating items are the ones in Part 16: the entity and ABN, the seeded config, and the first operator.
  2. A design audit is not a functional test. Part 7 was thorough and correct about what it looked at. It looked at rendering. The three scripts above close that gap and now run before every publish.
  3. Add KEA-24: "Ops portal walkthrough against a real booking" — this build verified the customer side end to end but the ops side (assign, collect, weigh, invoice, mark paid) has been exercised only by reading the code. Owner Callum, before the first paid job.
